NEW LATENT DEFECT — GAP-ASSETS-PAG-001: Cyberint assets endpoint has server-controlled page size. GetAssetsRequest declares page_number (integer, minimum 1, default 1) inline with no page_size field and no reference to the Pagination-Input schema (that schema belongs to GetAssetsTechnologiesRequest — a different endpoint). ADR-056 ratified PageNumber { page_size } variant cannot cover it: termination rule page_record_count < page_size requires a client-known page_size that this endpoint does not expose. Without pagination block, prism sends no page_number, server applies default page 1, all assets beyond page 1 are silently dropped — CWE-390 silent-truncation class, same category as CRIT-003. GetAssetsResponse.total_assets is the evidence field. Alerts surface unaffected (GetAlertsRequest has client-specifiable size with maximum 100; uses ratified page_number variant). Implementing engineer required by T-03 to leave a GAP-ASSETS-PAG-001 comment in TOML. (2) FB53a: orchestrator asserted CursorPagination was referenced by no endpoint (false; it is reached via $ref from GetCredentialsRequest.properties.pagination; scan searched only serialized operation objects and missed $ref indirection). Transferable lesson: a negative result from a pattern match is a statement about the pattern, not the corpus — schema claims must verify $ref resolution and key-prefix matching. | GAP-ASSETS-PAG-001 [new-latent-defect; D-2030/FB53c; owner: architect+orchestrator; status: OPEN — orchestrator-flagged, NOT yet authorized by human] | Cyberint assets endpoint (`POST /external/api/v1/assets/`) has server-controlled page size. `GetAssetsRequest` declares `page_number` (integer, minimum 1, default 1) inline with no `page_size` field and no reference to the `Pagination-Input` schema — that schema belongs to `GetAssetsTechnologiesRequest`, a different endpoint. Without a `[tables.steps.pagination]` block, prism sends no `page_number`, the server applies default page 1, and all assets beyond page 1 are silently dropped — CWE-390 silent-truncation class, same category as CRIT-003 on alerts. `GetAssetsResponse.total_assets` is the evidence field: if `total_assets > len(assets)` on the first response, later assets were missed. Alerts surface unaffected (`GetAlertsRequest` has client-specifiable `size` with maximum 100, uses ADR-056 ratified `page_number` variant). Implementing engineer required by T-03 in S-WAVE-A-CYBERINT-SPEC-001 to leave a `# GAP-ASSETS-PAG-001` comment in cyberint-assets.sensor.toml marking the gap as traceable at code level. | Architect: design a new `PaginationConfig` variant for server-controlled page size — termination driven by `total_assets` comparison or short/empty page detection rather than client-known `page_size`. Same blast-radius as ADR-056: new enum variant, three `pipeline.rs` dispatch sites (`build_paged_url_impl`, `build_request`, `execute_impl`), BC-2.16.002 postcondition row, new ADR. Follow-up story TBD — do NOT create story without explicit human authorization. | human authorization pending |

Architect confirmed empirically that `classify_predicates §classify_predicates` never inspects a predicate RHS — it extracts the column name, maps to `ColumnPushDownOption §ColumnPushDownOption`, and matches on the option alone; `WHERE device_id = ''` is indistinguishable from a non-empty value at plan time. That is correct by design: Tier 1 answers "is this REQUIRED column constrained at all" (structural, AST-level, no interpolation context); Tier 2 answers "is the value usable" at interpolation time via `required_filters §FetchStep` and E-SPEC-029 before any HTTP dispatch. Adding a redundant value check at Tier 1 was rejected as not clearing the production-grade bar. EC-007 added to S-REQUIRED-COL-GATE-001 documenting the NOT-A-DEFECT adjudication and the Tier-2 backstop. No AC or RGT required — no failing behavior exists. | RESOLVED D-2083 FB111 | RESOLVED D-2083 FB111 |

That is correct by design: Tier 1 answers "is this REQUIRED column constrained at all" (structural, AST-level, no interpolation context); Tier 2 answers "is the value usable" at interpolation time via `required_filters §FetchStep` and E-SPEC-029 before any HTTP dispatch — the layering guarantee holds for both arms.
